# Qubit source omissions — candidate work rows

Machine-generated by `scripts/source_inventory.py` from the pinned
Hermes `69948c005791d4d0e51670ca015238c2c7663749` (v0.21.5) and OpenClaw `da03996ab7203fe6b9b4d8edb2b54726e4394c9c` (v2026.4.19-beta.2-28254-gda03996ab7) source trees.

**Scope.** Inventory items are classified as **distinct capabilities** (no parent row) or **parented surfaces** (a CLI option/positional, model or provider entry, plugin asset/API/test/manifest, OpenClaw extension, workflow source file, or dashboard component whose capability already has a ledger row). A parented surface whose parent row is `IMPLEMENTED` is covered by the parent and is **not** a standalone gap. Cross-cutting evidence (i18n keys, locale keys, upstream tests) is counted, not enumerated per key.

Matching is mechanical: exact ledger id for CLI (`cli:<name>`), routes (`api:<METHOD> <path>`), and dashboard routes (`dashboard:<path>`); otherwise a boundary-aware name/path search across every ledger row. Parent ids are resolved against the live merged ledger and the nearest declared ancestor is used when an exact `cli:` subcommand row is absent.

## Fork-runtime dispositions for tools / skills / plugins (1379)

### `openclaw extension acpx` (1; IMPLEMENTED 0, GATED 1)

- `openclaw-ext:acpx` — **GATED**; backend `voicebridge/acp_client.py HARNESS_CAPS/adapters`; OpenClaw extension 'acpx' -> wave-6 audit: the ACP harness *client* contract is real; the acpx harness runtime is not shipped, so execution has no transport. (parent=OC-58, gate=ACP_HARNESS_UNAVAILABLE).

### `openclaw extension active-memory` (1; IMPLEMENTED 1, GATED 0)

- `openclaw-ext:active-memory` — **IMPLEMENTED**; backend `voicebridge/active_memory.py recall/resolve/status (bounded session policy over voicebridge/memory/store.py)`; OpenClaw extension 'active-memory' -> wave-6 audit: active-recall layer over the existing MemoryStore: bounded policy, scored recall, prompt-injection resolve, typed escalation; Qubit reaches it from CLI and dashboard. (parent=OC-D04, gate=none).; proof `tests/test_active_memory8u.py`

### `openclaw extension alibaba` (1; IMPLEMENTED 0, GATED 1)

- `openclaw-ext:alibaba` — **GATED**; backend `voicebridge/media_gen.py shape_media_request/generate`; OpenClaw extension 'alibaba' -> wave-6 audit: media provider gated on an operator credential + adapter. (parent=OC-64, gate=MEDIA_GEN_UNAVAILABLE).; proof `tests/test_media_gen8u.py`

### `openclaw extension amazon-bedrock` (1; IMPLEMENTED 1, GATED 0)

- `openclaw-ext:amazon-bedrock` — **IMPLEMENTED**; backend `qubit_cli.providers:get_provider`; 'amazon-bedrock' resolves through qubit_cli.providers.get_provider(), so the provider capability the openclaw extension supplies is served by Qubit's own provider plugin.; proof `tests/test_cli_agent1_families.py`
